idea: Fully autonomous; both roasts (design roast and PR roast) on. Scope: herdr-graph side of the approved design docs/reviews/2026-10-29-seat-review-H2-identity-design.md (read also docs/reviews/2026-10-29-seat-review-H2.md and docs/reviews/2026-10-29-seat-review-H2-identity.md). Includes: H2 Option D (bead hg-s6o: /seat drops accept_command, skill step 5 delegates acceptance to herdr-threads); clone.threads_seat write-once binding; participation keyed to the clone/threads seat (not occupant), kept across agent exit (D3); agent launch/resume sets HERDR_GRAPH_CLONE=<cl_>; delete clone re-matching in favour of SeatInspect; undo/resurrection via threads UnretireSeat + relaunch with key; G4 headless resume driver; tab/workspace matching by preserved Herdr ids. Threads-side work (G1 v2.8.2 release, G2, D1 continuity key, D2 reversible retirement with requirement restore, G5) is owned by the herdr-threads coordinator (seat snSGTpBk7xGd, thread dOqG4VQNjQ4); graph tasks that depend on unreleased threads features must be sequenced/blocked on them, not faked. Constraints: Herdr is not modified; do not touch the user's live graph instance or live Herdr layout; another session may be editing src/cli/seat.rs and skills — check main is current before starting and rebase as needed.

- finding-source · {"version":1,"source":"roast-code/round-1","artifact":"docs/superpowers/runs/2026-11-04-threads-seat-identity/2026-11-05-threads-seat-identity-roast-pr-1.md","kind":"roast","verdict":"Should-fix (7 confirmed) [low coverage] [review readiness incomplete]","occurrences":[{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":1,"key":"[Should-fix] src/threads/effects.rs:545","text":"[Should-fix] src/threads/effects.rs:545 — Under the seat-keyed rule, a pre-upgrade Required invitation that the old occupant-keyed code had already moved to Released (or Retired) counts as satisfied once the backfill records the same seat, so the clone is never re-invited to its required seat/teamspace channels. [lanes: data-migrations]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: head `(Some(i), InviteConstraint::Required) => same_seat(i)` checks only link.seat == threads_seat and ignores state. Base released Required rows whenever `link.occupant != occupant`, and the release executor (effects.rs ~1620-1630) records `state: Released` while keeping the link and its seat. Backfill assigns threads_seat to that same seat, so the row reads satisfied; the head cleanup loop only releases Pending/Accepted rows and threads_seat is write-once. The only migration test (`upgrade_with_links_on_the_backfilled_seat_causes_no_reinvite`, threads/tests.rs:878) seeds only accepted and pending states. Design §3 (\"links whose seat equals the backfilled threads_seat are satisfied\") assumes live links.\n  fix-shape hint: treat a Required row in Released/Retired state as unsatisfied (re-invite once) during backfill, or have the backfill reset such rows; add a migration test seeding a Released Required row."},{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":2,"key":"[Should-fix] src/threads/assignment.rs:80; src/threads/assignment.rs:101","text":"[Should-fix] src/threads/assignment.rs:80; src/threads/assignment.rs:101 — Purge writes Finalize tombstones for every seated clone, but nothing in production derives a finalize want or admits TombstoneDone, so tombstones stay in the store indefinitely and every clone consumer must filter them out. [lanes: simplicity-design, data-migrations]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: `purge_tombstone` returns `SeatIntent::Finalize` whenever `threads_seat` is Some; `ClonePurge` is a registered operator command (core_kinds.rs:1894/1925). `git grep` of non-test src finds `SeatTransition::TombstoneDone` only at its match arm (assignment.rs:269) and no finalize want in effects.rs. Design §8 says \"A clone whose seat is already final (every seat before D2) is purged at once, with no intent\" and §14.2 says \"Purge always writes a tombstone after D2\"; bead hg-66u.31 (finalize/unretire wants, D2) is still open. Every pre-D2 seat is final, so the tombstones never leave the store and `!is_tombstone()` filters are spread across seat_observation.rs, effects.rs, assignment.rs, core_kinds.rs, clone_status.rs and undo/preview.rs.\n  fix-shape hint: delete the record outright when no finalize consumer exists (the spec's pre-D2 rule), or defer the tombstone write to the D2 bead that lands the FinalizeRetirement want."},{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":3,"key":"[Should-fix] src/reconcile/herdr_exec.rs:571","text":"[Should-fix] src/reconcile/herdr_exec.rs:571 — A Restore launch whose MarkLaunched admission fails is turned into ExecOutcome::Obsolete with no log, no notice and no stuck_restores entry, so the restored clone silently never starts. [lanes: observability]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: herdr_exec.rs ~571-583 drops the WriterError and returns Obsolete; reconcile/mod.rs:909 handles Obsolete with only `self.mark(Obsolete)` (no eprintln, no last_error, no mark_attention, unlike Failed at :895). The effect id is deterministic (`mk_as(epoch, 0, rev, StartAgent, clone)`), and `Reconciler::upsert` (mod.rs:547) skips any id the journal already knows, so the launch is never re-planned for that epoch; `launched` stays false so `stuck_restores` (launched: true only) never names it. Spec lines 359-360 and 397-398 require a failed attempt to be surfaced in status. The ground seat's \"a later planner pass can re-emit\" is answered by the reproduce seat's mod.rs:547 citation; the refute seat's \"rejection means the intent changed\" covers stale-epoch rejections but not a writer error on an unchanged intent.\n  fix-shape hint: return `Failed(msg)` carrying the writer error for non-stale-epoch failures, or surface the restore as stuck when the mark fails."},{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":4,"key":"[Nit] src/observe/seat_observation.rs:149","text":"[Nit] src/observe/seat_observation.rs:149 — In SeatObservation's Resolved pass, a clone displaced by an earlier clone's Resolved answer is pushed into `out.unbound` and stays there when the same loop later binds it to its own pane, so it is committed Unknown and stripped from the baseline matches while bound in `matches`. [lanes: correctness]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: `unbind(m, &mut out, &other)` pushes the displaced clone into `out.unbound` and nothing removes it on re-insert; step.rs ~549 `append_seat_ops` emits `availability_request(.., Unknown)` for every `out.unknown()` clone with a binding, and step.rs ~624 strips every `seat_out.unbound` clone from `bm`. Needs a pane swap or collision between two seated clones; lasts one step and self-heals.\n  fix-shape hint: remove a clone from `out.unbound` when the Resolved loop re-binds it."},{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":5,"key":"[Nit] src/model/clone.rs:37","text":"[Nit] src/model/clone.rs:37 — Rollback is lossy: a base binary silently drops the new CloneRecord fields (threads_seat, threads_seat_history, seat_intent, seat_fact) when it rewrites a record, with no schema bump or rollback note. Refute seat REJECT citing bead hg-66u.17 (\"Configuration smoke: live handoff and cold restart on a private rig\", files tests/e2e_private_herdr.rs and tests/threads_real.rs) as OPEN and gated behind hg-66u.36 (\"Gate: fix loop exited\", which holds the smokes so they measure the final SHA), with a documented read-only fallback. The two CONFIRMs do not address the bead (the ground seat says \"nothing in the diff records that deferral\"), and the seat evidence does not settle whether this branch may merge before hg-66u.17 closes. A human should decide: if the smoke is a merge gate, confirm it as Should-fix and run hg-66u.17 after the fix loop; if it is deliberately sequenced after this roast, record that deferral in the run and treat the absence as expected."},{"section":"Escalations the reporter omitted (engine-added — escalate routes are final)","position":1,"key":"src/reconcile/planner.rs:173; src/reconcile/planner.rs:171","text":"src/reconcile/planner.rs:173; src/reconcile/planner.rs:171 — LiveIndex::bound_pane matches a clone's committed pane id in any incarnation without checking the pane's token, so the resume driver and the StartAgent executor fallback can start a clone's resumed session (with its env identity) in a pane that now belongs to someone else after a restart, and no test covers a reused id resolving to another clone's pane. (external-unverified)"},{"section":"Escalations the reporter omitted (engine-added — escalate routes are final)","position":2,"key":"src/observe/matcher.rs:212; src/reconcile/planner.rs:366; src/reconcile/planner.rs:204","text":"src/observe/matcher.rs:212; src/reconcile/planner.rs:366; src/reconcile/planner.rs:204 — Tab and workspace matching (matcher.rs and LiveIndex::workspace/tab/tab_owner) now link a bound Herdr id by equality in any incarnation with no token or Herdr-version check, so a reused id after a restart lets a stale binding claim an unrelated tab or workspace that the reconciler then acts on. (external-unverified)"}]}

This contradicts spec section 13 (\"A Finalize tombstone is not planned, ... resurrected\").\n  - At head, src/plan/core_kinds.rs: the `revived` loop in `SeatResurrect::plan` (around line 1576) and `revives_clone` in `SeatResurrect::mutate` (around line 1606) test only `lifecycle == Retired && retired_with(..)`. The same applies to the teamspace-resurrect plan loop around line 827.\n  - Effect: when the purged clone was the seat's only clone retired with it, `seat resurrect --active` previews a `clone.resurrect` and an `open_pane` for the tombstone. It then skips the \"create a fresh clone\" branch. `do_resurrect_seat` correctly skips the tombstone, so the commit leaves an active seat with no active clone. This was reproduced by the probe above.\n  - The cluster's own test `seat_resurrect_skips_a_purged_clone` does not catch this, because a live sibling is always revived."},{"section":"Findings","position":2,"key":"Name resolution includes tombstones. `grammar::resolve_clone(.., Scope::Retired)` matches tombstones by name. So `clone purge <name>`, run after a same-named clone was purged and a new one retired, fails as ambiguous and lists the inert tombstone as a candidate. Separately, an ambiguous retired-name match falls through to the live lookup, so with a live clone of the same name the operator gets \"retire it first\" instead of the ambiguity. Both cases only refuse; the id form works.","text":"Name resolution includes tombstones. `grammar::resolve_clone(.., Scope::Retired)` matches tombstones by name. So `clone purge <name>`, run after a same-named clone was purged and a new one retired, fails as ambiguous and lists the inert tombstone as a candidate. Separately, an ambiguous retired-name match falls through to the live lookup, so with a live clone of the same name the operator gets \"retire it first\" instead of the ambiguity. Both cases only refuse; the id form works."},{"section":"Findings","position":3,"key":"Before D2, purge of every seated clone writes a `Finalize{seat}` tombstone. Spec section 8 says \"A clone whose seat is already final (every seat before D2) is purged at once, with no intent\", but 14.2 (\"after D2\"), which supersedes it, together with hg-66u.35's description, prescribes the tombstone always. No finalize want exists until hg-66u.31 (D2-gated, open), so these tombstones persist until then. 14.2 makes a read showing the seat final count as acknowledgement, so they will drain once that want lands. They are inert meanwhile: excluded from status, resurrect, undo, and the one-live-clone-per-seat check.","text":"Before D2, purge of every seated clone writes a `Finalize{seat}` tombstone. No finalize want exists until hg-66u.31 (D2-gated, open), so these tombstones persist until then. 14.2 makes a read showing the seat final count as acknowledgement, so they will drain once that want lands. They are inert meanwhile: excluded from status, resurrect, undo, and the one-live-clone-per-seat check."},{"section":"Findings","position":4,"key":"The `ReleaseKey` branch cannot be reached at head. - finding-source · {"version":1,"source":"inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe/cluster-1","artifact":"reviews/inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe/final-review-inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe.cluster-1.md","kind":"final-review","verdict":"ready","occurrences":[{"section":"Findings","position":1,"key":"Membership lifetime is documented in its post-D2 form without a caveat. `CURRENT-DESIGN.md`","text":"Membership lifetime is documented in its post-D2 form without a caveat. `CURRENT-DESIGN.md`\n  (\"memberships end at purge\") and `DESIGN-NOTES.md:331` (\"last until purge\") state it as the\n  contract. At this head D2 has not been released, and spec section 14.10 says a graph retire always\n  closes the clone's pane. Every pane-close retirement is final before D2 (section 2), so threads\n  ends the seat's memberships when the clone is retired, not when it is purged. CURRENT-DESIGN is\n  the authorized contract, so this does not block, but readers of the current state will be misled\n  until D2 lands."},{"section":"Findings","position":2,"key":"`CURRENT-DESIGN.md` says a seatless clone's record is removed at purge; `GRAPH_STATE.md` says a","text":"`CURRENT-DESIGN.md` says a seatless clone's record is removed at purge; `GRAPH_STATE.md` says a\n  seatless clone with a key reservation keeps a `ReleaseKey` tombstone. Both describe real phases:\n  production purge passes `reservation = None` (`src/plan/core_kinds.rs:1892,1923`, \"hg-66u.13\n  passes the recorded key reservation\"), so the CURRENT-DESIGN wording is correct only before D1.\n  This is a wording gap, not a behavior defect."},{"section":"Findings","position":3,"key":"Cross-cluster note for the seam/plan reviewer, not a defect in this cluster: spec section 8 says a","text":"Cross-cluster note for the seam/plan reviewer, not a defect in this cluster: spec section 8 says a\n  clone whose seat is already final (\"every seat before D2\") is purged at once with no intent, and\n  section 14.2 limits \"always writes a tombstone\" to after D2. At this head `purge_tombstone` writes\n  `Finalize{seat}` for every seated clone. No production code emits `SeatTransition::TombstoneDone`\n  (only tests do; the finalize want is hg-66u.31, gated on D2), so before D2 every purged seated\n  clone stays as a tombstone indefinitely. CURRENT-DESIGN documents the implemented behavior\n  (\"until threads finalizes the retirement\"), so the doc and the code agree with each other but not\n  with spec section 8's pre-D2 sentence."},{"section":"Findings","position":4,"key":"The \"Threads 2 node identity and reviewed recovery\" heading still reads \"updated 2026-10-18\"","text":"The \"Threads 2 node identity and reviewed recovery\" heading still reads \"updated 2026-10-18\"\n  although its body now names threads 3.0.0. This is cosmetic."},{"section":"Untested scope","position":1,"key":"No test exercises the claim in CURRENT-DESIGN.md that `status` shows retired-unpurged clones at `clones.retired_unpurged` end to end. `clone_status_lists_retired_unpurged_clones` (src/plan/tests.rs:3599) calls `clone_status` directly, not through the daemon's `clones` status provider.","text":"No test exercises the claim in CURRENT-DESIGN.md that `status` shows retired-unpurged clones at `clones.retired_unpurged` end to end. `clone_status_lists_retired_unpurged_clones` (src/plan/tests.rs:3599) calls `clone_status` directly, not through the daemon's `clones` status provider."},{"section":"Untested scope","position":2,"key":"The v3.0.0 pin compatibility was checked here only for lock consistency (`cargo metadata --locked`). Build, unit tests and `tests/threads_real.rs` against v3.0.0 belong to the adapter cluster and were not re-run in this review.","text":"The v3.0.0 pin compatibility was checked here only for lock consistency (`cargo metadata --locked`). Build, unit tests and `tests/threads_real.rs` against v3.0.0 belong to the adapter cluster and were not re-run in this review."},{"section":"Deferred OK","position":1,"key":"CURRENT-DESIGN.md and DESIGN-NOTES.md describe the post-D2 membership lifetime (\"end at purge\") with no pre-D2 caveat; add one, or revisit when the D2 gate (hg-66u.2) closes.","text":"CURRENT-DESIGN.md and DESIGN-NOTES.md describe the post-D2 membership lifetime (\"end at purge\") with no pre-D2 caveat; add one, or revisit when the D2 gate (hg-66u.2) closes."},{"section":"Deferred OK","position":2,"key":"CURRENT-DESIGN.md omits the ReleaseKey tombstone for a seatless clone with a key reservation; correct before D1, update with hg-66u.43/hg-66u.13.","text":"CURRENT-DESIGN.md omits the ReleaseKey tombstone for a seatless clone with a key reservation; correct before D1, update with hg-66u.43/hg-66u.13."},{"section":"Deferred OK","position":3,"key":"Seam/plan cluster to confirm: before D2, purge writes a Finalize tombstone for every seated clone, against spec section 8 (\"purged at once, with no intent\"), and nothing in production clears it until hg-66u.31.","text":"Seam/plan cluster to confirm: before D2, purge writes a Finalize tombstone for every seated clone, against spec section 8 (\"purged at once, with no intent\"), and nothing in production clears it until hg-66u.31."},{"section":"Deferred OK","position":4,"key":"The \"updated 2026-10-18\" date on the CURRENT-DESIGN.md threads identity heading is stale after the 3.0.0 edit.","text":"The \"updated 2026-10-18\" date on the CURRENT-DESIGN.md threads identity heading is stale after the 3.0.0 edit."}]}
- finding-source · {"version":1,"source":"inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe/cluster-10","artifact":"reviews/inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe/final-review-inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe.cluster-10.md","kind":"final-review","verdict":"not ready (LazyMap drops the threads call guard before seat_for/resolve_seat/inspect reach threads, regressing the base guard on seat_for)","occurrences":[{"section":"Must fix before landing","position":1,"key":"src/daemon/compose.rs:914-978: `LazyMap::map()` drops `_guard` (the shared `threads-calls.lock` flock plus epoch check) before the external call. As a result `seat_for` (guarded at base), `resolve_seat` (a threads write that feeds the write-once `threads_seat`) and `inspect` all reach threads unguarded, which breaks the namespace.rs:127 contract and lets a `ThreadsReconnect` commit mid-call. Keep the guard alive across each delegated call.","text":"src/daemon/compose.rs:914-978: `LazyMap::map()` drops `_guard` (the shared `threads-calls.lock` flock plus epoch check) before the external call. As a result `seat_for` (guarded at base), `resolve_seat` (a threads write that feeds the write-once `threads_seat`) and `inspect` all reach threads unguarded, which breaks the namespace.rs:127 contract and lets a `ThreadsReconnect` commit mid-call. Keep the guard alive across each delegated call."},{"section":"Untested scope","position":1,"key":"No test shows that a `LazyMap` call holds `threads-calls.lock` for its whole duration, for example a test where an exclusive holder blocks `seat_for`/`resolve_seat`/`inspect`. Such a test would have caught the guard regression.","text":"No test shows that a `LazyMap` call holds `threads-calls.lock` for its whole duration, for example a test where an exclusive holder blocks `seat_for`/`resolve_seat`/`inspect`. Such a test would have caught the guard regression."},{"section":"Untested scope","position":2,"key":"The clear-on-success branch of `LazyMap::inspect`'s `seat_view_error` slot is not exercised, because it needs a service that answers `SeatInspect`. Only the Rejected path is tested, and that path is simulated through an explicit-endpoint error rather than a real older service answering `Unsupported`.","text":"The clear-on-success branch of `LazyMap::inspect`'s `seat_view_error` slot is not exercised, because it needs a service that answers `SeatInspect`. Only the Rejected path is tested, and that path is simulated through an explicit-endpoint error rather than a real older service answering `Unsupported`."},{"section":"Untested scope","position":3,"key":"No composed-daemon test checks that the `clones` status component is registered and reachable through `status`. Only `clone_status` itself is tested, in src/plan/tests.rs.","text":"No composed-daemon test checks that the `clones` status component is registered and reachable through `status`. Only `clone_status` itself is tested, in src/plan/tests.rs."},{"section":"Untested scope","position":4,"key":"The `clones` component's `store.head()` error branch and the error swallowed by `all_clones(...).unwrap_or_default()` are not tested.","text":"The `clones` component's `store.head()` error branch and the error swallowed by `all_clones(...).unwrap_or_default()` are not tested."},{"section":"Deferred OK","position":1,"key":"`seat_view_error` is also filled by endpoint-setup `Rejected` errors (bad explicit endpoint, namespace recovery, claim_host), which the field's doc describes as an older service refusing the seat contract. The message itself stays accurate.","text":"`seat_view_error` is also filled by endpoint-setup `Rejected` errors (bad explicit endpoint, namespace recovery, claim_host), which the field's doc describes as an older service refusing the seat contract. The message itself stays accurate."},{"section":"Deferred OK","position":2,"key":"`clone_status` hides a store read error behind an empty `retired_unpurged` list. It could report `{\"error\": ...}` the way the `store.head()` branch does.","text":"`clone_status` hides a store read error behind an empty `retired_unpurged` list. It could report `{\"error\": ...}` the way the `store.head()` branch does."},{"section":"Deferred OK","position":3,"key":"`Services::new` defaults `seat_view` to `FakeThreadsSeatView` in non-test code. This is harmless (it never speaks, and `from_env` overrides it) and consistent with the exported `FakeThreads`.","text":"`Services::new` defaults `seat_view` to `FakeThreadsSeatView` in non-test code. This is harmless (it never speaks, and `from_env` overrides it) and consistent with the exported `FakeThreads`."},{"section":"Deferred OK","position":4,"key":"`ThreadsReconnect` never rewrites `threads_seat` or `seat_intent` (src/threads/reconnect.rs has no reference to either), so seat ids from the old node outlive a reconnect. This is outside this cluster and predates the guard regression; it is noted for the seam reviewer."}]}

- finding-source · {"version":1,"source":"inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe/cluster-18","artifact":"reviews/inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe/final-review-inv-4aa37d70-023c-483a-b47c-3dc801a586fe.cluster-18.md","kind":"final-review","verdict":"not ready (B7 pre-D2 succession want absent from ThreadsSource; B9/B10 gated and undelivered)","occurrences":[{"section":"Findings","position":1,"key":"src/threads/effects.rs clone_wants: an Active clone whose graph seat or teamspace is Dormant (seat deactivate, src/plan/core_kinds.rs ~1257, leaves clones Active) gets membership_seat = None, so `desired` is empty and the release loop `continue`s; its Pending/Accepted Required memberships are never released. Base released them (occupant_of returned None for an inactive clone). The code comment and spec §3 say a requirement is released when \"the want is gone\"; this case contradicts both and has no test. Impact is limited if deactivation also closes the panes (threads then final-retires the seat and ends memberships), but the graph's records stay Accepted.","text":"src/threads/effects.rs clone_wants: an Active clone whose graph seat or teamspace is Dormant (seat deactivate, src/plan/core_kinds.rs ~1257, leaves clones Active) gets membership_seat = None, so `desired` is empty and the release loop `continue`s; its Pending/Accepted Required memberships are never released. Base released them (occupant_of returned None for an inactive clone). The code comment and spec §3 say a requirement is released when \"the want is gone\"; this case contradicts both and has no test. Impact is limited if deactivation also closes the panes (threads then final-retires the seat and ends memberships), but the graph's records stay Accepted."},{"section":"Findings","position":2,"key":"src/threads/mapping.rs ThreadsSeatMap::inspect: one per-seat error aborts the whole batch. herdr-threads v3.0.0 answers SeatInspect for an unknown seat with NotFound (store/queries.rs seat_inspect), which api_error maps to Rejected; per the spec callers then \"log and change nothing\", so a single stale threads_seat (threads store reset, seat row removed) would freeze seat observation for every clone. Not observed in a run; inferred from the code (already recorded as an unverified assumption on hg-66u.8).","text":"src/threads/effects.rs membership_seat for retired clones: on v3.0.0 every pane-close retirement is final and threads ends the seat's memberships; a retired-unpurged clone with a Pending ordinary seat-wide invitation is polled, can settle as Released/Retired, and then reads as an unsatisfied Ordinary want, so an Invite against a final-retired seat is issued and fails (Rejected -> Failed in port_outcome). Not observed in a run; inferred from the code (already recorded as an unverified assumption on hg-66u.8)."},{"section":"Findings","position":4,"key":"The one-live-clone-per-seat invariant is enforced only inside ThreadsSeatMutation (Assign, Succeed); the spec says \"a commit that would violate it is rejected by the writer's validator\", and no writer-level validator exists, so an undo/resurrection commit reactivating a clone whose seat another live clone now holds is not rejected. The undo commit is outside this cluster; flagged for the seam reviewer.","text":"The one-live-clone-per-seat invariant is enforced only inside ThreadsSeatMutation (Assign, Succeed); the spec says \"a commit that would violate it is rejected by the writer's validator\", and no writer-level validator exists, so an undo/resurrection commit reactivating a clone whose seat another live clone now holds is not rejected. B9: no continuity key is reserved or emitted (`agent_env(&clone, false)` at every start site in src/reconcile/herdr_exec.rs), and hg-66u.43, .44, .33 and .68 are open. B10: threads does not restore memberships; they are re-invited once after succession, because D2 is gated. B4 and B5 for fresh agents and bare panes after a cold restart: hg-66u.21 is open. The diff's `occupancy()` ends occupancy only for matched clones in surviving panes, and a clone of a Dormant seat has no desired pane. Possible failure: an occupant that survives deactivation (for example the agent was killed with the tab and its SessionEnd hook never reported) would make reactivation clear the Restore with no launch, and the ordinary start rule also skips a clone with an occupant. The clone would then sit agentless with nothing surfaced in status. Possible failure: an occupant that survives deactivation (for example the agent was killed with the tab and its SessionEnd hook never reported) would make reactivation clear the Restore with no launch, and the ordinary start rule also skips a clone with an occupant. The clone would then sit agentless with nothing surfaced in status. This follows the spec's restore rule (\"never launched fresh\" before D1), but it extends that rule to a command the spec did not cover. The extension rests on an orchestrator decision in hg-66u.80, not a human row.","text":"[Minor][behavior-change] After hg-66u.80, `seat activate` of a seated clone with no resumable ended session (harness without resume, or no ended session on record) is recorded as `restore_stuck: not_launchable` instead of being started fresh as before this branch. scopeFilter-round-2: [Should-fix] src/threads/mapping.rs:161; src/threads/mapping.rs:172 in-scope — Incorrect behavior in a goal-named path: a service-wide refusal is treated as a per-seat answer, so clones are marked lost and unbound, which misreports the seat's authority over where a clone is and whether it is live.
scopeFilter-round-2: [Nit] tests/daemon_composed.rs:1882 in-scope — Weak test for a goal-named behavior: the live-handoff recovery test can pass even if the handoff causes a re-invite.
scopeFilter-round-2: [Nit] CURRENT-DESIGN.md:65 punch-list — Doc drift only. The docs describe stale purge/tombstone behavior, but the head's behavior is not wrong, so this is not a correctness defect in goal-named code.
scopeFilter-round-2: [Must-fix] B3 undelivered (and B1 partial): the graph's clone re-matching is not delegated to threads at head; observe/matcher.rs clone passes and the planner LiveIndex rebind and live_ref re-matching still run; hg-66u.14 and hg-66u.15 are open behind gate hg-66u.1 (D1); no human scope change row covers this. in-scope — unrouted by filter — defaulted in-scope
scopeFilter-round-2: [Must-fix] B9 undelivered: no continuity-key reservation or keyed launch exists; every agent start calls agent_env(&clone, false) in src/reconcile/herdr_exec.rs, so HERDR_THREADS_CONTINUITY_KEY is never exported; hg-66u.43, hg-66u.44, hg-66u.68, hg-66u.33 and hg-66u.40 are open behind D1. in-scope — unrouted by filter — defaulted in-scope
scopeFilter-round-2: [Must-fix] B7 and B10 undelivered: graph undo does not un-retire the seat or restore memberships hands-off; the pre-D2 interim (src/threads/effects.rs succeed_seat) allocates a successor seat and needs one re-acceptance; hg-66u.31 and hg-66u.32 are open behind gate hg-66u.2 (D2). in-scope — unrouted by filter — defaulted in-scope
scopeFilter-round-2: [Must-fix] B4 partial: the fresh-agent restart path (hg-66u.21) is open behind D1, so a clone with no resumable session is not relaunched unattended after a cold restart. in-scope — unrouted by filter — defaulted in-scope
scopeFilter-round-2: [Must-fix] B4 seam, PLAUSIBLE (src/reconcile/planner.rs plan_resume and restart_covered): the restart arm requires occupant None and an ended session. After a real headless cold restart in which Herdr drops metadata tokens and reallocates terminal ids (spec Problem description), the matcher does not match the clone, SeatObservation stays silent on Unresolved before D1, and diff::occupancy never ends the stale occupant. The arm then never fires and the agent is not resumed. All resume tests end the session via test_end_occupant and restart with keep_metadata=true. Verify on a private rig, or end the occupant once an Unresolved seat fact is read after an incarnation change. in-scope — unrouted by filter — defaulted in-scope
scopeFilter-round-2: [Must-fix] Seam, PLAUSIBLE (src/observe/seat_observation.rs seated_clones and observe): Active clones of a deactivated (Dormant) seat or teamspace are still inspected. plan_closes closes their tab or workspace as a predicted induced close, and threads retires those pane seats (spec §8). The Retired arm then emits an observed pane-close cascade that retires the clones, without checking seat lifecycle or predictions, so reactivation finds them retired instead of recreating them. No test covers deactivation with a seated clone. in-scope — unrouted by filter — defaulted in-scope
